# 全部内置节点索引

> 自动生成于 ComfyUI `0.33.0` 本地源码；共记录 **639** 个成功注册的非 API 内置节点。
> 不包含 `custom_nodes/` 第三方节点和 `comfy_api_nodes/` 云端/API 节点。节点随版本、可选依赖和硬件变化。

## 如何使用

- 浏览器或编辑器中按 `Ctrl/Cmd + F` 搜索节点英文显示名或内部类名。
- `required` 必须提供；`optional` 可不接；`hidden` 由运行时提供。
- `COMBO` 是下拉选择；模型文件类选项会根据本机目录动态变化。
- 源码说明保留英文原文，避免自动翻译扭曲模型专用语义；中文用途提示用于快速判断类别。
- 高频节点的中文接线和参数解释见 [基础核心节点逐项详解](01-基础核心节点逐项详解.md)。

## 生成范围

- 成功载入：`639` 个节点。
- 未完整初始化的 extra 模块：nodes_replacements.py（无服务端的导出环境未注册旧节点迁移规则；该模块不声明新节点，不影响节点数量）。
- 前端专用的 Reroute、Note、Group 等不在 Python 注册表内，见 [如何读懂任何节点](00-如何读懂任何节点.md)。

## 3d

处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Create 3D Animation File

- **内部类名**：`BuildPoseFile`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create 3D Animation File` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Build an animated GLB from pose data, or save a BVH mocap file.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA,KIMODO_POSE_DATA` | required | 源码提示：3D pose data. |
| `format` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：Output format, both fed to Save 3D Model to write to disk. 'glb' = animated GLB (mesh / bones / openpose / scail). 'bvh' = BVH mocap clip (one skeleton; needs the model). |
| `fps` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 24.0；最小 1.0；最大 240.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Animation frame rate. |
| `camera_translation` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'off'；源码提示：Bake pred_cam_t into the root's translation 'off' = bind position 'centered' = delta from frame 0 'absolute' = raw (Z is camera depth — usually meters away). |
| `track_index` | `INT` | required | 默认 -1；最小 -1；最大 15；源码提示：-1 = all tracks; ≥0 = single track. |
| `sam3d_body_model` | `SAM3D_BODY_MODEL` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Create 3D File (from Mesh)

- **内部类名**：`MeshToFile3D`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_save_3d`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create 3D File (from Mesh)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Serialize a mesh to a GLB File3D object for Save / Preview 3D nodes, carrying its UVs, colors, normals, texture, normal/occlusion/emissive maps and material. Supports one item per batch only.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D_GLB` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Create Camera Info

- **内部类名**：`CreateCameraInfo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create Camera Info` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Build a camera_infoMode 'orbit' aims with yaw/pitch/distance around the target; 'look_at' places the camera at world position. Coordinates are the viewer's world space (right-handed,Y-up).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：How to define the camera: orbit angles, an explicit position, or a position + quaternion. |
| `target_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -1000.0；最大 1000.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Look-at point (orbit pivot / aim). In orbit mode, move it to pan/translate the whole camera. Ignored in quaternion mode. Defaults to the origin. |
| `target_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -1000.0；最大 1000.0；步长 0.01 |
| `target_z` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -1000.0；最大 1000.0；步长 0.01 |
| `roll` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -180.0；最大 180.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Camera roll about the view axis, degrees. |
| `fov` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 35.0；最小 1.0；最大 120.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Vertical field of view in degrees. |
| `zoom` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.01；最大 100.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Digital zoom (focal-length multiplier). &gt;1 zooms in without moving the camera. |
| `camera_type` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：Projection used by Render Splat: perspective (foreshortening) or orthographic (parallel). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Load 3D & Animation

- **内部类名**：`Load3D`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_load_3d`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：加载对应资源并把它交给下游节点；具体对象见节点名 `Load 3D & Animation`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_file` | `COMBO` | required | — |
| `image` | `LOAD_3D` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 1；最大 4096；步长 1 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 1；最大 4096；步长 1 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |
| `mask` | `MASK` |
| `mesh_path` | `STRING` |
| `normal` | `IMAGE` |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` |
| `recording_video` | `VIDEO` |
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D` |
| `model_3d_info` | `LOAD3D_MODEL_INFO` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

- **源码说明（原文）**：Preview a 3D model file without saving it to the ComfyUI output directory.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_file` | `STRING,FILE_3D_GLB,FILE_3D_GLTF,FILE_3D_FBX,FILE_3D_OBJ,FILE_3D_STL,FILE_3D_USDZ,FILE_3D` | required | 默认 ''；源码提示：3D model file or path string |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` | optional | — |
| `bg_image` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| — | 无公开输出 |

- **源码说明（原文）**：Preview a gaussian splat 3D file without saving it to the ComfyUI output directory.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D_SPLAT_ANY,FILE_3D_PLY,FILE_3D_SPLAT,FILE_3D_SPZ,FILE_3D_KSPLAT` | required | 源码提示：A gaussian splat 3D file. |
| `viewport_state` | `LOAD_3D` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 1；最大 4096；步长 1 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 1；最大 4096；步长 1 |
| `model_3d_info` | `LOAD3D_MODEL_INFO` | optional | — |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` | optional | — |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D_SPLAT_ANY` |
| `model_3d_info` | `LOAD3D_MODEL_INFO` |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` |
| `width` | `INT` |
| `height` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

<a id="3d-mesh"></a>

## 3d/mesh

处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Decimate Mesh

- **内部类名**：`DecimateMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Decimate Mesh` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Simplifies a mesh to a target face count using QEM, on the active compute device. 'midpoint' is the cumesh-faithful preset (best quality, preserves thin features / hair); 'qem' places vertices at the QEM optimum with line/feature-edge controls. Output stays welded.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `target_face_count` | `INT` | required | 默认 200000；最小 0；最大 50000000；源码提示：Target max faces. 0 disables. |
| `placement_mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：midpoint: cumesh-faithful (recommended). qem: QEM-optimal placement. |
| `unique_id` | `UNIQUE_ID` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Fill Holes

- **内部类名**：`FillHoles`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Fill Holes` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Fills holes up to a max perimeter, preserving existing geometry/UVs. GPU-vectorised with auto-corrected winding and loop-averaged centroid colors

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `max_perimeter` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.03；最小 0.0；步长 0.0001；源码提示：Max hole perimeter to fill. 0 disables. |
| `weld_epsilon_rel` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1e-05；最小 0.0；步长 1e-06；源码提示：Pre-weld tolerance (fraction of bounding box diagonal); boundary detection needs welded vertices. 0 skips. |
| `max_vertices` | `INT` | required | 默认 16；最小 3；最大 1024；源码提示：Cap boundary vertices per cycle; centroid-fan only works for small near-planar holes. Keep ≤16. |
| `fill_chains` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Also fill open chains (not just cycles). Noisy; OFF matches cumesh. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Remesh Mesh (Narrow-Band DC)

- **内部类名**：`RemeshMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Remesh Mesh (Narrow-Band DC)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Re-extracts a uniformly tessellated mesh via a narrow-band distance field + Dual Contouring, on the active compute device. Normalizes messy / non-manifold / self-intersecting topology; run before Decimate Mesh to hit an exact face count. Output stays welded.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 512；最小 32；最大 2048；源码提示：Voxel grid resolution (output density). 256 ~ 100k faces, 512 ~ 1M. For an exact face count, follow with Decimate Mesh. |
| `sign_mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：udf: robust to messy/non-manifold input. sdf: clean single surface with QEF (Quadratic Error Function) sharp-feature recovery, but needs consistent winding. |
| `band` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.5；最大 4.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Narrow-band width in voxel units. In UDF mode also offsets the surface. |
| `project_back` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Linearly interpolate vertices toward the original surface (0 = pure DC, 1 = snapped). |
| `fix_poles` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Collapse valence-3 vertex pairs (DC T-junction artifact). |
| `smooth_iters` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 20；源码提示：Taubin smoothing iterations (0 = off). 2-3 cleans DC staircase-like artifacts; higher over-smooth QEF edges. |
| `drop_small_components` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.01；最小 0.0；最大 0.5；步长 0.005；源码提示：Drop components below this fraction of the largest's face count. 0 disables. |
| `precluster_max_verts` | `INT` | required | 默认 20000000；最小 0；最大 100000000；源码提示：Cap input vertex count before the field queries, inputs above this are cluster-decimated to it first. Prevents OOM on huge meshes. |
| `unique_id` | `UNIQUE_ID` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Render Mesh

- **内部类名**：`RenderMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Render Mesh`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Ray-casts a single view of a mesh. The camera and optional model transform can come from a Load3D / Preview3D viewer.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `mode` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：What to render. auto: texture if present, else vertex colours, else shaded clay. |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 4096；步长 8 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 4096；步长 8 |
| `background` | `COLOR` | required | 默认 '#000000' |
| `model_3d_info` | `LOAD3D_MODEL_INFO` | optional | 源码提示：Model transform from the same Load3D / Preview3D viewer. Connect it with camera_info to match the viewer framing. |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` | optional | 源码提示：Camera from a Load3D / Preview3D viewer or a Create Camera Info node. If none is connected, a default front view is auto-framed. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |
| `mask` | `MASK` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Rotate Mesh

- **内部类名**：`RotateMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_save_3d`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：对输入执行反相、翻转或旋转变换；具体对象见节点名 `Rotate Mesh`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Rotate a mesh. Euler XYZ applies X then Y then Z about the world axes (degrees). Quaternion is (w, x, y, z), auto-normalized.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Smooth Mesh Normals

- **内部类名**：`MeshSmoothNormals`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Smooth Mesh Normals` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Compute smooth per-vertex normals and attach them to the mesh. Meshes without normals are shaded flat (per-face) by glTF viewers; this makes them shade smoothly. With crease_angle below 180, edges sharper than the threshold are kept hard by splitting vertices along them.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `crease_angle` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 180.0；最小 0.0；最大 180.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Edges whose dihedral angle exceeds this value (degrees) stay hard (vertices are split). 180 = fully smooth; lower preserves sharp edges (e.g. ~30-60 for hard-surface). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Weld Vertices

- **内部类名**：`WeldVertices`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Weld Vertices` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Merge coincident vertices via L_inf grid quantization. Use when a mesh comes in unwelded (per-face verts, no shared edges) — pre-pass before FillHoles, DecimateMesh, or any topology-aware op. Colors averaged per cluster.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `epsilon_rel` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1e-05；最小 0.0；步长 1e-06；源码提示：Weld tolerance (fraction of bbox diagonal). 1e-5 for float dedup; 1e-3 for visibly-close-but-distinct verts. |
| `epsilon_abs` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；步长 1e-06；源码提示：Absolute weld tolerance (overrides epsilon_rel when &gt; 0). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。


<a id="3d-splat"></a>

## 3d/splat

处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Create 3D File (from Splat)

- **内部类名**：`SplatToFile3D`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create 3D File (from Splat)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Serialize a gaussian splat to a File3D object for Save / Preview 3D nodes. Supports one item per batch only.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` | required | — |
| `format` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：ply: standard 3D Gaussian Splat with full spherical harmonics. ksplat: mkkellogg SplatBuffer (level 0, uncompressed), base color only spz: Niantic gzip-compressed (~10x smaller), base color only |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D_SPLAT_ANY` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Extract Mesh from Splat

- **内部类名**：`SplatToMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：检测或提取输入中的结构化信息；具体对象见节点名 `Extract Mesh from Splat`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Extract a coloured mesh from a gaussian splat.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 384；最小 64；最大 768；步长 16；源码提示：Density-grid resolution along the longest axis. Higher = finer surface, more VRAM/time (grows with resolution^3). |
| `kernel` | `INT` | required | 默认 5；最小 1；最大 8；源码提示：Max splat half-width in voxels. Each gaussian is rasterized over a window sized to its own 3-sigma, capped here - small surfels stay cheap, large ones aren't truncated. Raise if sparse splats leave gaps. |
| `smooth` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 60；源码提示：Taubin mesh-smoothing iterations. Smooths the surface without shrinking it (volume-preserving), unlike blurring the density. 0 = raw surface. |
| `level` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.4；最小 0.0；最大 2.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Iso-surface level. Auto-picked by Otsu; this biases it (1.0 = auto, lower = fatter/more-connected surface, higher = thinner/tighter). |
| `min_component` | `INT` | required | 默认 500；最小 0；最大 100000；步长 50；源码提示：Drop connected components smaller than this many vertices (0 = keep all). Removes detached floater blobs and the inner shell of the double wall. |
| `min_opacity` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.02；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Ignore gaussians fainter than this before meshing. |
| `color_sharpen` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 2.0；最小 1.0；最大 8.0；步长 0.5；源码提示：Crisp up the vertex texture: 1.0 = physically-correct blend; higher biases each voxel's colour toward its dominant gaussian instead of averaging neighbours (de-smears the texture). Colour only - geometry is unchanged. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Get Splat

- **内部类名**：`File3DToSplat`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Get Splat` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Parse a splat File3D into a gaussian splat. Inverse of Create 3D File (from Splat). Supported format:  PLY, SPLAT, KSPLAT, SPZ. PLY carries full spherical harmonics, the other formats are base color only. Format is auto-detected from the file contents.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_3d` | `FILE_3D,FILE_3D_SPLAT_ANY,FILE_3D_PLY,FILE_3D_SPLAT,FILE_3D_KSPLAT,FILE_3D_SPZ` | required | 源码提示：A gaussian splat 3D file |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Get Splat Count

- **内部类名**：`GetSplatCount`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Get Splat Count` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Returns the number of splats summed across the batch.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` | required | — |
| `unique_id` | `UNIQUE_ID` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` |
| `count` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Merge Splats

- **内部类名**：`MergeSplat`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：合并、重组或复制批次数据；具体对象见节点名 `Merge Splats`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Concatenate any number of gaussian splats into one. Unioning several decodes of the same latent at different seeds densifies the surface, this can improve surface quality when meshing.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splats` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Render Splat

- **内部类名**：`RenderSplat`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Render Splat`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Render a gaussian splat as an image with an anisotropic EWA rasterizer (oriented elliptical splats, antialiased, depth-sorted front-to-back). The camera comes from a camera_info input (Load / Preview 3D, or a Create Camera Info node); leave it empty to auto-frame the splat. Set frames greater than 1 for a turntable batch of images to feed a Video node.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 2048；步长 8 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 2048；步长 8 |
| `frames` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 -240；最大 240；源码提示：-1, 0, 1 = single still image; &gt;1 = turntable, the camera orbits over a full 360 turn (works with any camera_info). Negative value orbits the other way. |
| `splat_scale` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.1；最大 5.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Multiplier on each splat's projected footprint (lower = crisper points, higher = softer/fuller surface). |
| `sharpen` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 2.0；最小 1.0；最大 8.0；步长 0.5；源码提示：Sharpen overlapping splats: 1.0 = physically-correct blend; higher biases each pixel toward its dominant (nearest) splat for crisper texture, without shrinking splats or opening gaps. Non-physical above 1. |
| `headlight_shading` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 3.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Diffuse shading from a light at the camera (headlight), using the splat surfel normals: darkens surfaces that turn away from view to reveal form/curvature. 0 = flat albedo, 1 = strongest shading. |
| `opacity_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Cull gaussians with opacity below this (removes faint floaters). |
| `render_style` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：What the image output shows: color, clay (neutral-albedo shaded), depth (near=bright), normal (OpenGL normal map). |
| `background` | `COLOR` | required | 默认 '#000000' |
| `bg_image` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Optional background plate composited behind the splat (overrides the solid background colour). Resized to the render size; a batch is used per frame, a single image for all. color/clay only. |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` | optional | 源码提示：Camera to render from - a Load3D / Preview3D camera or a Create Camera Info node. If empty, the splat is auto-framed from a default 3/4 view. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |
| `mask` | `MASK` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Transform Splat

- **内部类名**：`TransformSplat`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_gaussian_splat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Transform Splat` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Translate, rotate, and scale a gaussian splat. Non-uniform scale also reshapes every individual splat, slower process.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` | required | — |
| `translate_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -100.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `translate_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -100.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `translate_z` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -100.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `rotate_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -360.0；最大 360.0；步长 1.0 |
| `rotate_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -360.0；最大 360.0；步长 1.0 |
| `rotate_z` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -360.0；最大 360.0；步长 1.0 |
| `scale_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.01；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `scale_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.01；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `scale_z` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.01；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。


<a id="3d-texturing"></a>

## 3d/texturing

处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Apply Texture to Mesh

- **内部类名**：`ApplyTextureToMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把指定配置、条件或补丁应用到输入；具体对象见节点名 `Apply Texture to Mesh`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Attaches baked texture IMAGEs to a mesh's UV layout for SaveGLB. Feed the SAME mesh you baked

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `base_color` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `metallic` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |
| `roughness` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |
| `occlusion` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |
| `normal_map` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Bake Ambient Occlusion

- **内部类名**：`BakeAmbientOcclusion`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Bake Ambient Occlusion` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Bakes an ambient-occlusion map from a high-poly mesh into a low-poly's UV layout (white = open, dark = crevices). Feed the UV-unwrapped low_poly and the high_poly it was decimated from. Outputs a grayscale image for Apply Texture To Mesh's occlusion input (packed into the ORM map / occlusionTexture).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `low_poly` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `high_poly` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 8192；步长 64 |
| `samples` | `INT` | required | 默认 64；最小 4；最大 1024；步长 4；源码提示：Rays per texel. More = smoother, slower. Raise if grainy. |
| `max_distance` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.01；最大 2.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Ray length, as a fraction of the bounding box diagonal. Smaller = tighter, more local occlusion. |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 2.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Scales the occlusion. &gt;1 darkens, &lt;1 lightens. |
| `bias` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.01；最小 0.0001；最大 0.2；步长 0.0005；源码提示：Ray origin lift off the surface, as a fraction of the bounding box diagonal. Raise if even surfaces show dark blotches/holes. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `occlusion` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Bake Normal Map from Mesh

- **内部类名**：`BakeNormalMapFromMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Bake Normal Map from Mesh` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Bakes a tangent-space normal map (glTF/OpenGL +Y) from a high-poly mesh into a low-poly's UV layout, capturing detail lost during decimation. Feed the UV-unwrapped low_poly and the high_poly it was decimated from. Outputs an image for Apply Texture To Mesh's normal_map input.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `low_poly` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `high_poly` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 8192；步长 64 |
| `cage_distance` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.05；最小 0.001；最大 0.5；步长 0.001；源码提示：Surface search band, as a fraction of the bounding box diagonal. Raise for wrong/missing patches under heavy decimation; lower if it grabs across gaps. |
| `ignore_backfaces` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Skip high-poly surfaces facing away from the texel, so crevices/enclosed spaces don't grab the opposite wall. Disable only if the high-poly winding is inconsistent. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `normal_map` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Bake Texture From Voxel

- **内部类名**：`BakeTextureFromVoxel`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Bake Texture From Voxel` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Bakes PBR textures onto the mesh's existing UV layout (trilinear-sample the sparse voxel volume). Does NOT unwrap — connect a UV unwrap node upstream. Outputs base_color + metallic/roughness grayscale IMAGEs (black if no PBR); feed them to ApplyTextureToMesh (SAME mesh) for SaveGLB.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `voxel_colors` | `VOXEL` | required | — |
| `texture_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 2048；最小 64；最大 8192；源码提示：Square UV atlas resolution. |
| `reference_mesh` | `MESH` | optional | 源码提示：Optional dense pre-decimation mesh; back-projects each texel onto its true surface before sampling, removing faceted baking on coarse meshes. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `base_color` | `IMAGE` |
| `metallic` | `IMAGE` |
| `roughness` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Mesh Texture to Image

- **内部类名**：`MeshTextureToImage`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Mesh Texture to Image` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Extracts a mesh's baked textures as individual IMAGEs: base_color, metallic, roughness, occlusion and normal_map. Channels with nothing baked come back neutral (occlusion white, normal flat).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `base_color` | `IMAGE` |
| `metallic` | `IMAGE` |
| `roughness` | `IMAGE` |
| `occlusion` | `IMAGE` |
| `normal_map` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Render UV Atlas

- **内部类名**：`RenderUVAtlas`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Render UV Atlas`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Renders a mesh's UV layout as an image.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 4096；步长 64 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。

### Unwrap Mesh UVs

- **内部类名**：`UnwrapMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mesh_postprocess`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Unwrap Mesh UVs` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Generates a UV atlas: segments the surface into charts, parameterizes each, packs into a [0,1] atlas. Vertices on chart seams are duplicated (one copy per chart, same position, own UV), so the output mesh has more vertices than the input. Run after Remesh/Decimate, before texture baking.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` | required | — |
| `segmenter` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'pec'；源码提示：pec: fast parallel-edge-collapse charting on GPU. adaptive: CPU, slower. |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 0；最大 8192；步长 256；源码提示：Target atlas resolution for texel-density auto-scale (0 = fit-to-content). |
| `padding` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 0；最大 16；源码提示：Texel padding between charts. |
| `weld_distance` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.0001；源码提示：Coincident-vert merge radius as a fraction of mesh extent (0 = auto). Raise to ~0.001 if you get per-triangle charts (unwelded input). |
| `unique_id` | `UNIQUE_ID` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mesh` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理 3D、网格、点云或 splat 数据；使用前核对坐标系、单位、材质和导出格式。


<a id="advanced-debug"></a>

## advanced/debug

先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### EasyCache

- **内部类名**：`EasyCache`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_easycache`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `EasyCache` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Native EasyCache implementation.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model to add EasyCache to. |
| `reuse_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.2；最小 0.0；最大 3.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The threshold for reusing cached steps. |
| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.15；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The relative sampling step to begin use of EasyCache. |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.95；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The relative sampling step to end use of EasyCache. |
| `verbose` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to log verbose information. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.15；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The relative sampling step to begin use of LazyCache. |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.95；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The relative sampling step to end use of LazyCache. |
| `verbose` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to log verbose information. |

- **用途提示**：执行 `CFGNorm` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `pre_cfg` | `BOOLEAN` | optional | 默认 False；源码提示：If true, rescale the combined noise BEFORE the sampler's CFG combine, without clamping (can amplify). Matches the norm-scaled CFG used by models like Lens. Default false keeps the original post-CFG x0-space attenuate-only behavior. |

### LTXV Modality Guidance (A/V coupling)

- **内部类名**：`LTXVModalityGuidance`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lt`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `LTXV Modality Guidance (A/V coupling)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Cross-modal (audio-video) guidance for LTXV-AV. Runs one extra forward pass per step with the a2v/v2a cross-attention severed, then pushes the result toward the coupled prediction - strengthening audio-visual sync (e.g. lip-sync). Reference default modality_scale is 3.0. Stacks with the dual-CFG guider and STG. Set to 1.0 to disable (no extra pass).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `modality_scale` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 3.0；最小 1.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.1 |
| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001 |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### LTXV Spatio-Temporal Guidance (STG)

- **内部类名**：`LTXVSpatioTemporalGuidance`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lt`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `LTXV Spatio-Temporal Guidance (STG)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Runs one extra pass per step with the self-attention of the selected blocks degraded to a value-passthrough, then guides away from it - improving spatial detail and motion coherence.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `scale` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |
| `blocks` | `STRING` | required | 默认 '29'；源码提示：Comma-separated transformer block indices to perturb. |
| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001 |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### Normalized Attention Guidance

- **内部类名**：`NAGuidance`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_nag`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Normalized Attention Guidance` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Applies Normalized Attention Guidance to models, enabling negative prompts on distilled/schnell models.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model to apply NAG to. |
| `nag_scale` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 5.0；最小 0.0；最大 50.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：The guidance scale factor. Higher values push further from the negative prompt. |
| `nag_alpha` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Blending factor for the normalized attention. 1.0 is full replacement, 0.0 is no effect. |
| `nag_tau` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.5；最小 1.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

<a id="advanced-multigpu"></a>

## advanced/multigpu

先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### MultiGPU CFG Split

- **内部类名**：`MultiGPU_WorkUnits`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_multigpu`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `MultiGPU CFG Split` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Prepares model to have sampling accelerated via splitting work units.  Should be placed after nodes that modify the model object itself, such as compile or attention-switch nodes.  Other than those exceptions, this node can be placed in any order.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `max_gpus` | `INT` | required | 默认 2；最小 1；步长 1 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### Select CLIP Device

- **内部类名**：`SelectCLIPDevice`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_multigpu`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Select CLIP Device` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Place the CLIP text encoder on a specific device (default / cpu / gpu:N).  - "default" restores the device assigned by the loader. - "cpu" pins both the load and offload device to CPU. - "gpu:N" pins the load device to the Nth available GPU.  When the selected device does not exist on the current machine (e.g. a workflow built on a 2-GPU box opened on a 1-GPU box), the node passes the CLIP through unchanged and logs a message instead of failing.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `clip` | `CLIP` | required | — |
| `device` | `COMBO` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `CLIP` | `CLIP` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### Select Model Device

- **内部类名**：`SelectModelDevice`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_multigpu`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Select Model Device` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Place the diffusion model on a specific device (default / cpu / gpu:N).  - "default" restores the device assigned by the loader (even after a   prior Select Model Device call). - "cpu" pins both the load and offload device to CPU. - "gpu:N" pins the load device to the Nth available GPU; the offload   device is restored to the loader's original choice.  When the requested device differs from the device the input model is already on, a fresh model is spawned via the loader's reload factory (cached_patcher_init) so the new patcher owns independent weights on the new device. Loaders that don't support multigpu (no factory) will cause the node to pass through unchanged with a warning.  If the workflow already has MultiGPU CFG Split applied and the chosen GPU collides with one of the existing multigpu clones, that clone is dropped so two patchers don't end up bound to the same device.  When the selected device does not exist on the current machine (e.g. a workflow built on a 2-GPU box opened on a 1-GPU box), the node passes the model through unchanged and logs a message instead of failing.  NOTE: Placing Select Model Device *after* a node that has already consumed the same model (e.g. a KSampler that ran on this model on the original device) is not recommended -- any state the prior consumer mutated on the original model will be observed when the selected device matches the original (fast path). Place Select Model Device before any consumer of the model.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `device` | `COMBO` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

### Select VAE Device

- **内部类名**：`SelectVAEDevice`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_multigpu`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Select VAE Device` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Place the VAE on a specific device (default / gpu:N).  - "default" restores the device assigned by the loader. - "gpu:N" pins the load device to the Nth available GPU; the offload   device is set to the standard VAE offload device.  CPU is intentionally not exposed in the UI for the VAE; if a workflow supplies "cpu" anyway (e.g. opened from another machine), the request is dropped with a log message and the VAE is passed through unchanged.  When the selected device does not exist on the current machine (e.g. a workflow built on a 2-GPU box opened on a 1-GPU box), the node passes the VAE through unchanged and logs a message instead of failing.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `device` | `COMBO` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `VAE` | `VAE` |

**小白提醒**：先根据输入、输出和官方模板确认作用；模型专用节点不要脱离对应工作流单独套用。

## audio

- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_audio`
- **标记**：普通节点

- **源码说明（原文）**：Adjust the volume of the audio by a specified amount in decibels (dB).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `audio` | `AUDIO` | required | — |
| `volume` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 -100；最大 100；源码提示：Volume adjustment in decibels (dB). 0 = no change, +6 = double, -6 = half, etc |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `AUDIO` | `AUDIO` |

**小白提醒**：处理音频；重点核对采样率、声道、时长和批次，长音频会明显增加内存。

### Audio Equalizer (3-Band)

- **内部类名**：`AudioEqualizer3Band`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_audio`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Audio Equalizer (3-Band)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|

| `low_gain_dB` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -24.0；最大 24.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Gain for Low frequencies (Bass) |
| `low_freq` | `INT` | required | 默认 100；最小 20；最大 500；源码提示：Cutoff frequency for Low shelf |
| `mid_gain_dB` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -24.0；最大 24.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Gain for Mid frequencies |
| `mid_freq` | `INT` | required | 默认 1000；最小 200；最大 4000；源码提示：Center frequency for Mids |
| `mid_q` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.707；最小 0.1；最大 10.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Q factor (bandwidth) for Mids |
| `high_gain_dB` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -24.0；最大 24.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Gain for High frequencies (Treble) |
| `high_freq` | `INT` | required | 默认 5000；最小 1000；最大 15000；源码提示：Cutoff frequency for High shelf |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `AUDIO` | `AUDIO` |

**小白提醒**：处理音频；重点核对采样率、声道、时长和批次，长音频会明显增加内存。

- **源码说明（原文）**：Predicts the natural shot duration for a prompt using the LTX 2.4 duration head (loaded with ModelPatchLoader), and snaps it to the VAE's 8k+1 frame grid.

|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `duration_head` | `MODEL_PATCH` | required | 源码提示：LTX 2.4 duration head loaded with ModelPatchLoader. |
| `frame_rate` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 24.0；最小 1.0；最大 120.0；步长 0.01 |
| `min_seconds` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.5；最大 120.0；步长 0.1 |
| `max_seconds` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 20.0；最小 0.5；最大 120.0；步长 0.1 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `num_frames` | `INT` |
| `seconds` | `FLOAT` |

<a id="image"></a>

## image

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Add Layer

- **内部类名**：`AddLayer`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_compositor`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Add Layer` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：Layer content at its native size. A batch expands to consecutive layers. |
| `layers` | `LAYERS` | optional | 源码提示：Layer stack to append to. Leave unconnected to start a new stack. |
| `mask` | `MASK` | optional | 源码提示：Transparency mask for this layer. Masked areas (value 1) become transparent, multiplying with any alpha channel the image already carries. |
| `name` | `STRING` | optional | 默认 ''；源码提示：Layer name shown in the compositor editor. |
| `x` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 -16384；最大 16384；源码提示：Initial horizontal placement on the canvas. |
| `y` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 -16384；最大 16384；源码提示：Initial vertical placement on the canvas. |
| `opacity` | `FLOAT` | optional | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Initial layer opacity. |
| `blend_mode` | `COMBO` | optional | 默认 'normal'；源码提示：Initial blend mode, applied against the layers below. On the bottom layer over the default transparent background, non-normal modes produce transparency. |
| `rotation` | `FLOAT` | optional | 默认 0.0；最小 -360.0；最大 360.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Initial rotation in degrees, clockwise. |
| `width` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：Initial display width. 0 keeps the image's native width. |
| `height` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：Initial display height. 0 keeps the image's native height. |
| `z_index` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 -1000；最大 1000；源码提示：Stacking override. Layers are stable-sorted by z_index; equal values keep their list order. |
| `flip_h` | `BOOLEAN` | optional | 默认 False；源码提示：Flip the layer horizontally. |
| `flip_v` | `BOOLEAN` | optional | 默认 False；源码提示：Flip the layer vertically. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `LAYERS` | `LAYERS` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Create Layered Image

- **内部类名**：`ImageCompositor`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_compositor`
- **标记**：实验性；输出节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create Layered Image` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `layers` | `LAYERS` | required | 源码提示：Layer stack to composite; build it with Add Layer. Items are stacked by z_index, batch frames inside an item expand to consecutive layers, and item placement, opacity, and blend mode define the initial composition. Without an explicit document canvas the size is a best-effort maximum extent of the placed layers. A saved composition that matches the current inputs takes priority. |
| `compositor` | `COMPOSITOR` | required | 默认 {}；源码提示：Layered composition saved by the compositor editor. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |
| `MASK` | `MASK` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Layers From Bounding Boxes

- **内部类名**：`LayersFromBoundingBoxes`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_compositor`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Layers From Bounding Boxes` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Turn an image batch plus its bounding boxes into a layer stack, one layer per frame, each placed by its own box. Use this when a node emits layers as a batch - a batch carries a single placement for every frame, so the individual positions are otherwise lost.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：Image batch; each frame becomes one layer. |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX,ARRAY,STRING` | required | 源码提示：Placement boxes, index-aligned with the image batch. Accepts bounding boxes (x, y, width, height), normalized elements (with a 'bbox' - these need canvas_width/canvas_height to resolve to pixels), or a JSON string of either. Frames without a matching box are placed at the origin. A box's width/height scales the layer to fit it. metadata.name (or desc) and metadata.z_index are used when present, and metadata.content_rect (frame-relative) crops the frame to its real content. |
| `mask` | `MASK` | optional | 源码提示：Per-frame transparency, index-aligned with the image batch (1 = transparent, LoadImage convention). |
| `layers` | `LAYERS` | optional | 源码提示：Layer stack to append to. Leave unconnected to start a new stack. |
| `crop_to_content` | `BOOLEAN` | optional | 默认 True；源码提示：Crop each frame to metadata.content_rect where present and place the content at the box position plus the rect offset. Leave on for batches whose frames are padded - it keeps only the real content at its true spot. |
| `canvas_width` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：Document canvas width. 0 derives it from the placed layers. |
| `canvas_height` | `INT` | optional | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：Document canvas height. 0 derives it from the placed layers. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `LAYERS` | `LAYERS` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

| `images` | `IMAGE` |

<a id="image-detection"></a>

## image/detection

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Detect Face Landmarks (MediaPipe)

- **内部类名**：`MediaPipeFaceLandmarker`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mediapipe`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：检测或提取输入中的结构化信息；具体对象见节点名 `Detect Face Landmarks (MediaPipe)`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Detects facial landmarks using MediaPipe model.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `face_detection_model` | `FACE_DETECTION_MODEL` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `detector_variant` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'short'；源码提示：Face detector range. 'short' is tuned for close-up faces (within ~2 m of the camera); 'full' covers farther / smaller faces (up to ~5 m) but is slower. 'both' runs both detectors and keeps whichever found more faces per frame (~2× detection cost). |
| `num_faces` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 0；最大 16；步长 1；源码提示：Maximum faces to return per frame. 0 = no cap (return all detected). |
| `min_confidence` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：BlazeFace score threshold. Lower to catch small/occluded faces. |
| `missing_frame_fallback` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'empty'；源码提示：Per-frame behaviour when detection fails in a batch. 'empty' leaves the frame faceless. 'previous' copies the most recent successful detection. 'interpolate' lerps landmarks/bbox/blendshapes between bracketing successful frames. Multi-face: pairs faces across frames by greedy bbox-centre NN. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `face_landmarks` | `FACE_LANDMARKS` |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Draw BBoxes

- **内部类名**：`DrawBBoxes`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_rtdetr`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Draw BBoxes`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` | required | 默认 {'x': 0, 'y': 0, 'width': 512, 'height': 512} |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `out_image` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Draw Face Mask (MediaPipe)

- **内部类名**：`MediaPipeFaceMask`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mediapipe`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Draw Face Mask (MediaPipe)`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Draws a mask from face landmarks.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `face_landmarks` | `FACE_LANDMARKS` | required | — |
| `regions` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：'all' = union of face_oval+lips+eyes+irises (which collapses to face_oval since it encloses the rest). 'custom' = toggle each region individually for combos like lips+eyes. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MASK` | `MASK` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Face Expression to SAM3D Body

- **内部类名**：`SAM3DBody_FaceExpression`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Face Expression to SAM3D Body` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Drive MHR face blendshapes from the core MediaPipe Face Landmarker.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sam3d_body_model` | `SAM3D_BODY_MODEL` | required | — |
| `mhr_pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 4.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Global multiplier on all blendshapes. &gt;1 exaggerates. |
| `mouth_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 4.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Multiplier on mouth/jaw shapes. MediaPipe's jawOpen saturates near 1.0. |
| `eye_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 2.0；最小 0.0；最大 4.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Multiplier on eye shapes. MediaPipe rarely exceeds 0.5; 2-3x often needed. |
| `brow_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 2.0；最小 0.0；最大 4.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Multiplier on brow/cheek/sneer shapes. MediaPipe outputs ~0.1-0.3; 2-3x. |
| `input_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.02；最小 0.0；最大 0.5；步长 0.01；源码提示：Deadzone on MediaPipe's raw output (below = zero, above = linear remap). |
| `blendshape_smooth_window` | `INT` | required | 默认 7；最小 1；最大 31；步长 2；源码提示：Gaussian window on MediaPipe's per-frame signal before MHR mapping. MediaPipe's raw output swings 30-70% frame-to-frame on static faces. 1 = disabled. Use odd values. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mhr_pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

- **内部类名**：`SAM3DBody_Loader`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点

|---|---|---|---|
| `model_file` | `COMBO` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `sam3d_body_model` | `SAM3D_BODY_MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Render 3D Body Pose

- **内部类名**：`SAM3DBody_Render`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Render 3D Body Pose`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA,KIMODO_POSE_DATA` | required | 源码提示：MHR pose data, or external Y-up rig pose data (KimodoSample). All render styles work for external rigs that carry OpenPose joint maps in their _skeleton_override (KimodoSample does). |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；步长 8；源码提示：Output width in pixels. 0 = use pose data's native image_size. If only one of width/height is set, the other is derived preserving the original aspect. |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；步长 8；源码提示：Output height in pixels. 0 = use pose data's native image_size. If only one of width/height is set, the other is derived preserving the original aspect. |
| `render_style` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：'mesh' = 3D MHR mesh rasterized through the camera. 'silhouette' = binary mask of the mesh. 'openpose_2d' = flat 2D skeleton 'openpose_3d' = openpose skeleton as flat-shaded 3D model 'scail' = SCAIL 3D capsules |
| `background` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Per-frame background. Omitted = black canvas. |
| `camera_info` | `LOAD3D_CAMERA` | optional | 源码提示：Free 6DOF camera override. When wired, the pose is re-projected through this camera (position/target/zoom/rotation/FoV) instead of the predicted one. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Run SAM3 Video Track

- **内部类名**：`SAM3_VideoTrack`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run SAM3 Video Track` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：Video frames as batched images |
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `detection_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Score threshold for text-prompted detection. |
| `max_objects` | `INT` | required | 默认 4；最小 0；最大 64；源码提示：Max tracked objects. Initial masks count toward this limit. 0 uses the internal cap of 64. |
| `detect_interval` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；源码提示：Run detection every N frames (1=every frame). Higher values save compute. |
| `initial_mask` | `MASK` | optional | 源码提示：Mask(s) for the first frame to track (one per object) |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` | optional | 源码提示：Text conditioning for detecting new objects during tracking |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `track_data` | `SAM3_TRACK_DATA` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Run SAM3D Body Prediction

- **内部类名**：`SAM3DBody_Predict`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run SAM3D Body Prediction` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sam3d_body_model` | `SAM3D_BODY_MODEL` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `run_hand_refinement` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Improves hand pose at the cost of extra inference time and memory use |
| `fov` | `FLOAT` | required | 源码提示：Vertical FoV in degrees. Affects predicted depth and absolute scale. 0 = fall back to ~53° (16:9). |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 64；最小 1；最大 512；步长 1；源码提示：Max person crops to process as a batch. Larger values use more VRAM for faster inference. |
| `track_data` | `SAM3_TRACK_DATA` | optional | 源码提示：Tracking data from SAM3 Video Track, required for multi-person detection |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` | optional | 默认 {'x': 0, 'y': 0, 'width': 512, 'height': 512}；源码提示：Per-frame bounding boxes used for better detection. Can be used as an alternative to tracking data. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mhr_pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01 |
| `refine_iterations` | `INT` | required | 默认 2；最小 0；最大 5；源码提示：SAM decoder refinement passes (0=use raw detector masks) |
| `individual_masks` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Output per-object masks instead of union |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` | optional | 源码提示：Text conditioning from CLIPTextEncode |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` | optional | 默认 {'x': 0, 'y': 0, 'width': 512, 'height': 512}；源码提示：Bounding boxes to segment within |
| `positive_coords` | `STRING` | optional | 源码提示：Positive point prompts as JSON [{"x": int, "y": int}, ...] (pixel coords) |
| `negative_coords` | `STRING` | optional | 源码提示：Negative point prompts as JSON [{"x": int, "y": int}, ...] (pixel coords) |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `masks` | `MASK` |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Smooth SAM3D Body Pose Data

- **内部类名**：`SAM3DBody_Smooth`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sam3d_body`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Smooth SAM3D Body Pose Data` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Reduce frame-to-frame jitter via temporal smoothing.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mhr_pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA` | required | — |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.05；源码提示：Smoothing strength. 0 = raw, 1 = smoothed. |
| `method` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'savgol'；源码提示：gaussian: symmetric weighted average, best general-purpose smoother. savgol: sliding polynomial fit, preserves sharp peaks. |
| `window` | `INT` | required | 默认 7；最小 1；最大 51；步长 2；源码提示：Temporal window in frames (odd values). |
| `rotation_threshold_degrees` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 30.0；最小 0.0；最大 90.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Disables smoothing for this root rotation rate (degree/frame) to preserve fast spins. 30° suits most content, low values might disable smoothing on ordinary jitter and silently impacts quality. 0 = disable. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `mhr_pose_data` | `MHR_POSE_DATA` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Visualize Face Landmarks (MediaPipe)

- **内部类名**：`MediaPipeFaceMeshVisualize`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_mediapipe`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Visualize Face Landmarks (MediaPipe)`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Draws face landmarks mesh on the input image.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `face_landmarks` | `FACE_LANDMARKS` | required | — |
| `connections` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：'all' = oval+eyes+brows+lips+irises+nose. 'fill' = solid face_oval polygon (silhouette mask). 'custom' = toggle each feature individually (including 'tesselation', the full 2547-edge wireframe). |
| `color` | `COLOR` | required | 默认 '#00ff00' |
| `thickness` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 0；最大 8；步长 1；源码提示：Edge line thickness in pixels. 0 disables edge drawing. |
| `point_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 2；最小 0；最大 16；步长 1；源码提示：Landmark dot radius in pixels. 0 disables point drawing. |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：If not connected, a black canvas will be used. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

<a id="image-geometry-estimation"></a>

## image/geometry estimation

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Convert DA3 Geometry to Mesh

- **内部类名**：`DA3GeometryToMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_depth_anything_3`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Convert DA3 Geometry to Mesh` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Convert a depth map into a triangulated 3D mesh.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `da3_geometry` | `DA3_GEOMETRY` | required | — |
| `batch_index` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 4096；源码提示：Which image of a batch to convert. Per-image vertex counts differ so batches cannot be stacked. |
| `decimation` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 8；源码提示：Vertex stride. 1 = full resolution, 2 = half, etc. |
| `discontinuity_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.04；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Drop triangles whose 3x3 depth span exceeds this fraction. 0 = off. |
| `confidence_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.1；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Exclude pixels whose per-image normalised confidence is below this value (0 = keep all, 1 = keep only the single most confident pixel). Used when the geometry has a confidence map (Small/Base models). |
| `use_sky_mask` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Exclude sky-probability pixels (sky &gt;= 0.5) from the mesh. Used when the geometry has a sky map (Mono/Metric models). |
| `texture` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Use the source image as a base color texture. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MESH` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Convert MoGe Point Map to Mesh

- **内部类名**：`MoGePointMapToMesh`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_moge`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Convert MoGe Point Map to Mesh` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Convert a MoGe point map into a 3D mesh.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `moge_geometry` | `MOGE_GEOMETRY` | required | — |
| `batch_index` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 4096；源码提示：Which image of a batched MoGe geometry to mesh. Per-image vertex counts differ, so batches can't be stacked into a single MESH. |
| `decimation` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 8；源码提示：Vertex stride; 1 = full resolution. |
| `discontinuity_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.04；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Drop pixels whose 3x3 depth span exceeds this fraction. 0 = off. |
| `texture` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Carry the source image through as the baseColor texture. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MESH` | `MESH` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

|---|---|---|---|
| `da3_geometry` | `DA3_GEOMETRY` | required | — |
| `output` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：- depth: normalised greyscale depth image. - depth_colored: depth mapped through the Turbo colormap. - sky_mask: sky probability in [0, 1] (for Mono/Metric models only). - confidence: normalised depth confidence (for Small/Base models only). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Render MoGe Geometry

- **内部类名**：`MoGeRender`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_moge`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把数据绘制或渲染为可观察结果；具体对象见节点名 `Render MoGe Geometry`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Render a depth map or normal map from geometry data

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `moge_geometry` | `MOGE_GEOMETRY` | required | — |
| `output` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'depth'；源码提示：DirectX vs OpenGL controls the normal-map green-channel convention. DirectX: green = -Y down (Unreal). OpenGL: green = +Y up (Blender, Substance, Unity, glTF). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Run Depth Anything 3

- **内部类名**：`DA3Inference`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_depth_anything_3`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run Depth Anything 3` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Run Depth Anything 3 on an image. In multi-view mode each image is treated as a separate view of the same scene.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `da3_model` | `DA3_MODEL` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 504；最小 140；最大 2520；步长 14；源码提示：Resolution the model runs at (longest side, multiple of 14). Lower = faster / less VRAM. Higher = more detail. Output is upsampled back to the original size. |
| `resize_method` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'upper_bound_resize'；源码提示：upper_bound_resize: scale so the longest side = resolution (caps memory, default). lower_bound_resize: scale so the shortest side = resolution (preserves more detail on tall/wide images, uses more memory). |
| `mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：mono: single view image (works with any model variant). multiview: all images processed together for geometric consistency + camera pose (for Small/Base models only). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `da3_geometry` | `DA3_GEOMETRY` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Run MoGe Inference

- **内部类名**：`MoGeInference`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_moge`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run MoGe Inference` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Run MoGe on a single image to estimate depth and geometry.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `moge_model` | `MOGE_MODEL` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `resolution_level` | `INT` | required | 默认 9；最小 0；最大 9；源码提示：0 = fastest, 9 = most detail. |
| `fov_x_degrees` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 170.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Horizontal field of view of the source camera. Sets the focal length used to unproject the depth map into 3D. 0 = auto-recover from the predicted points. |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 4；最小 1；最大 64；源码提示：Images per inference call. Lower if you OOM on a long video / image set. |
| `force_projection` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True |
| `apply_mask` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Set masked-out (sky / invalid) pixels to inf in points and depth so meshing culls them. Disable to keep the raw predicted geometry everywhere; the mask is still returned separately. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `moge_geometry` | `MOGE_GEOMETRY` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Run MoGe Panorama Inference

- **内部类名**：`MoGePanoramaInference`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_moge`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run MoGe Panorama Inference` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Run MoGe on an equirectangular panorama by splitting it into 12 perspective views, running inference on each, and merging the results into a single depth map.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `moge_model` | `MOGE_MODEL` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：Equirectangular panorama (any aspect). |
| `resolution_level` | `INT` | required | 默认 9；最小 0；最大 9；源码提示：Per-view detail (0 = fastest, 9 = most detailed). |
| `split_resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 512；最小 256；最大 1024；源码提示：Resolution of each perspective split. |
| `merge_resolution` | `INT` | required | 默认 1920；最小 256；最大 8192；源码提示：Long-side resolution of the merged equirect distance map. |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 4；最小 1；最大 12；源码提示：Views per inference batch (12 splits total). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `moge_geometry` | `MOGE_GEOMETRY` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

<a id="image-post-processors"></a>

## image/post-processors

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Post-Process SeedVR2 Output

- **内部类名**：`SeedVR2PostProcessing`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_seedvr`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Post-Process SeedVR2 Output` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Align the generated image with the original resized image and apply color correction.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：The generated image to process. |
| `original_resized_images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：The original resized image before pre-processing, used as reference. |
| `color_correction_method` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'lab'；源码提示：Method to match the generated image colors to the original image. lab: transfer color in CIELAB space, preserving detail (most faithful). wavelet: transfer low-frequency color, keeping upscaled high-frequency detail. adain: match per-channel mean/std (fastest, global tint). none: skip color transfer (geometry alignment only). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

<a id="image-shader"></a>

## image/shader

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### GLSL Shader

- **内部类名**：`GLSLShader`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_glsl`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `GLSL Shader` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Apply GLSL ES fragment shaders to images. u_resolution (vec2) is always available.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fragment_shader` | `STRING` | required | 默认 '#version 300 es\nprecision highp float;\n\nuniform sampler2D u_image0;\nunifor…；多行文本；源码提示：GLSL fragment shader source code (GLSL ES 3.00 / WebGL 2.0 compatible) |
| `size_mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：Output size: 'from_input' uses first input image dimensions, 'custom' allows manual size |
| `images` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | 源码提示：Images are available as u_image0-4 (sampler2D) in the shader code |
| `floats` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | 源码提示：Floats are available as u_float0-19 in the shader code |
| `ints` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | 源码提示：Ints are available as u_int0-19 in the shader code |
| `bools` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | 源码提示：Booleans are available as u_bool0-9 (bool) in the shader code |
| `curves` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | required | 源码提示：Curves are available as u_curve0-3 (sampler2D, 1D LUT) in the shader code. Sample with texture(u_curve0, vec2(x, 0.5)).r |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE0` | `IMAGE` |
| `IMAGE1` | `IMAGE` |
| `IMAGE2` | `IMAGE` |
| `IMAGE3` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。


<a id="image-transform"></a>

## image/transform

处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

### Crop By Bounding Boxes

- **内部类名**：`CropByBBoxes`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_sdpose`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：裁剪输入的空间范围或时间范围；具体对象见节点名 `Crop By Bounding Boxes`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Crop and resize regions from the input image batch based on provided bounding boxes.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` | required | 默认 {'x': 0, 'y': 0, 'width': 512, 'height': 512} |
| `output_width` | `INT` | required | 默认 512；最小 64；最大 4096；步长 8；源码提示：Width each crop is resized to. |
| `output_height` | `INT` | required | 默认 512；最小 64；最大 4096；步长 8；源码提示：Height each crop is resized to. |
| `padding` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 1024；步长 1；源码提示：Extra padding in pixels added on each side of the bbox before cropping. |
| `keep_aspect` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'stretch'；源码提示：Whether to stretch the crop to fit the output size, or pad with black pixels to preserve aspect ratio. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理像素图像；重点核对宽高、批次、颜色通道和 alpha。

| `degrade_sigma` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：0 = clean latent. Increase to denoise corrupted latent outputs. |

## model/conditioning/bernini

构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### Bernini Conditioning

- **内部类名**：`BerniniConditioning`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_bernini`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Bernini Conditioning` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Conditioning node for Bernini in-context video/image conditioning. It can be used for the following tasks: t2v (text-to-video), v2v (video-to-video), rv2v (reference-guided video editing), r2v (reference-to-video), ads2v (insert image/video into video).Reference images injected as in-context tokens (r2v, rv2v) are encoded independently at their own native aspect ratio (long edge capped at ref_max_size)

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 832；最小 16；最大 8192；步长 16 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 480；最小 16；最大 8192；步长 16 |
| `length` | `INT` | required | 默认 81；最小 1；最大 8192；步长 4 |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 4096 |
| `source_video` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Source video to edit or restyle (v2v, rv2v). Resized to width/height and trimmed to length. |
| `reference_video` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Video to insert into the source video (ads2v). |
| `reference_images` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | optional | — |
| `ref_max_size` | `INT` | optional | 默认 848；最小 16；最大 8192；步长 16；源码提示：Max size for the long edge of reference_video and reference_images. Resized with preserved aspect ratio and snapped to 16px. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### Get IC-LoRA Parameters

- **内部类名**：`GetICLoRAParameters`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lt`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Get IC-LoRA Parameters` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Extracts IC-LoRA parameters from the safetensors metadata of a LoRA-loaded model and outputs them for LTXVAddGuide (eg. reference_downscale_factor).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `iclora_model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：Direct output from a LoRA Loader for the specific IC-LoRA from which to extract the metadata. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `iclora_parameters` | `IC_LORA_PARAMETERS` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### LTXV Reference Audio (ID-LoRA)

- **内部类名**：`LTXVReferenceAudio`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lt`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `LTXV Reference Audio (ID-LoRA)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Set reference audio for ID-LoRA speaker identity transfer. Encodes a reference audio clip into the conditioning and optionally patches the model with identity guidance (extra forward pass without reference, amplifying the speaker identity effect).

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `reference_audio` | `AUDIO` | required | 源码提示：Reference audio clip whose speaker identity to transfer. ~5 seconds recommended (training duration). Shorter or longer clips may degrade voice identity transfer. |
| `audio_vae` | `VAE` | required | 源码提示：LTXV Audio VAE for encoding. |
| `identity_guidance_scale` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 3.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Strength of identity guidance. Runs an extra forward pass without reference each step to amplify speaker identity. Set to 0 to disable (no extra pass). |
| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001；源码提示：Start of the sigma range where identity guidance is active. |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001；源码提示：End of the sigma range where identity guidance is active. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### LTXVAddGuide

- **内部类名**：`LTXVAddGuide`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lt`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `LTXVAddGuide` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `latent` | `LATENT` | required | — |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：Image or video to condition the latent video on. Must be 8*n + 1 frames. If the video is not 8*n + 1 frames, it will be cropped to the nearest 8*n + 1 frames. |
| `frame_idx` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 -9999；最大 9999；源码提示：Frame index to start the conditioning at. For single-frame images or videos with 1-8 frames, any frame_idx value is acceptable. For videos with 9+ frames, frame_idx must be divisible by 8, otherwise it will be rounded down to the nearest multiple of 8. Negative values are counted from the end of the video. |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01 |
| `attention_mask` | `MASK` | optional | 源码提示：Optional pixel-space spatial mask. Controls per-region conditioning influence via self-attention, multiplied by strength. |
| `iclora_parameters` | `IC_LORA_PARAMETERS` | optional | 源码提示：Optional IC-LoRA parameters from a Get IC-LoRA Parameters node. Used for adjusting guide processing as required by certain IC-LoRAs (eg. those with a reference_downscale_factor &gt; 1). When chained, each LTXVAddGuide uses only the parameters connected to it. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

- **源码说明（原文）**：Anchor an image, a short clip, audio, or a clip with its soundtrack at any frame of a MiniMax H3 video. Chain several nodes to anchor several frames.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |

| `vae` | `VAE` | optional | 源码提示：Video VAE, needed when an image is connected. |
| `audio_vae` | `VAE` | optional | 源码提示：Audio VAE, needed when an audio is connected. |
| `image` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Image or video frames to anchor. Multi-frame batches are anchored as a clip and cropped down to the model's valid clip lengths: 5, 22, 39... (17k + 5) frames. Batches shorter than 5 frames use only the first image. |
| `audio` | `AUDIO` | optional | 源码提示：Soundtrack to anchor starting at the same frame index, cropped to the video's remaining duration. |

| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 768；最小 32；最大 16384；步长 32 |
| `length` | `INT` | required | 默认 124；最小 5；最大 3600；步长 17；源码提示：Frame count at 24 fps, snapped up to the model's 17k+5 grid (124 = ~5s; trained range is ~124-362, longer is untested) |
| `first_frame` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |
| `last_frame` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

### MiniMax H3 Reference to Video

- **内部类名**：`MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_minimax_h3`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `MiniMax H3 Reference to Video` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：&lt;Picture i&gt; / &lt;Video k&gt; / &lt;Audio j&gt; reference conditioning for MiniMax H3. Use the same tags when prompting.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `clip` | `CLIP` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `audio_vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `prompt` | `STRING` | required | 多行文本 |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1344；最小 32；最大 16384；步长 32 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 768；最小 32；最大 16384；步长 32 |
| `length` | `INT` | required | 默认 124；最小 5；最大 3600；步长 17；源码提示：Frame count at 24 fps, (124 = ~5s, trained range is ~124-362) |
| `ref_image_size` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'match'；源码提示：Reference image sizing. 'match' scales each ref (down only, keeping aspect) to the generation's pixel area; 'max' uses the reference pipeline's 2048px short edge for best identity fidelity. Reference tokens ride through every sampling step, so 'max' can be several times slower. |
| `ref_images` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | optional | — |
| `ref_videos` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | optional | — |
| `ref_video_audios` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | optional | — |
| `ref_audios` | `COMFY_AUTOGROW_V3` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

| `size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 256；最大 4096；步长 16；源码提示：Square image size. The model is trained at 1024; other sizes run but are off-distribution. |
