<a id="model-conditioning-wan-animate"></a>

## model/conditioning/wan/animate

构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### WanAnimate2Cache

- **内部类名**：`WanAnimate2Cache`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_wan`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `WanAnimate2Cache` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Caches the pose-video's per-block activations so it runs once instead of on every sampling step. Roughly halves generation time Tradeoff is ~12.5 GB of system RAM at 480x832/81 frames in bf16 (scales with resolution and length). With context windows each window is cached separately, so RAM scales with the window count; use the static_standard schedule, as uniform schedules shift the windows every step and nothing ever recurs to hit the cache.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `device` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'cpu'；源码提示：Where to keep the cache. cpu (RAM) is the safe choice, the cache will not fit in VRAM alongside the model at typical sizes. gpu (VRAM) can be faster if it fits. |
| `dtype` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'default'；源码提示：Storage precision. default stores the activations in the model's compute dtype. int8 halves the cache, int4 quarters it, convrot is used to retain accuracy. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### WanAnimate2ToVideo

- **内部类名**：`WanAnimate2ToVideo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_wan`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `WanAnimate2ToVideo` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Animate a character in a reference image using a video, effectively replicating the facial animation, body motion and hands gesture from the video.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 832；最小 16；最大 16384；步长 16；源码提示：Output video width in pixels. |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 480；最小 16；最大 16384；步长 16；源码提示：Output video height in pixels. |
| `length` | `INT` | required | 默认 81；最小 1；最大 16384；步长 4；源码提示：Number of frames to generate. |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 4096；源码提示：Number of videos to generate simultaneously. |
| `video_frame_offset` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；步长 1；源码提示：Frames to seek into the pose video. Connect to the video_frame_offset output of the previous node when extending. |
| `pose_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Scales the pose video's influence on the motion. 1.0 is the trained behavior; below weakens adherence, above amplifies. 0.0 mutes it but does not fully remove it. |
| `pose_start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Sampling percent at which the pose influence starts. Outside the window the pose branch is skipped entirely, which also speeds those steps up. |
| `pose_end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Sampling percent at which the pose influence ends. Motion is mostly established early, so e.g. 0.7 can loosen fine detail while keeping the choreography. |
| `reference_image_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Scales how strongly generated frames attend to the reference image's latent frame. Below 1.0 loosens identity/appearance adherence (e.g. to let the prompt restyle), above tightens it against drift. |
| `reference_image` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：The character to animate. |
| `pose_video` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：The video whose motion is transferred to the reference character. |
| `clip_vision_output` | `CLIP_VISION_OUTPUT` | optional | 源码提示：CLIP vision of the reference image. |
| `positive_pose` | `CONDITIONING` | optional | 源码提示：Prompt for the pose-video branch, describing the motion rather than the character. Defaults to positive. Used for both the cond and uncond passes. |
| `clip_vision_output_pose` | `CLIP_VISION_OUTPUT` | optional | 源码提示：CLIP vision of the pose video's first frame. Defaults to clip_vision_output. |
| `continue_motion` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Previous motion sequence to continue from for temporal consistency. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |
| `trim_latent` | `INT` |
| `trim_image` | `INT` |
| `video_frame_offset` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### Generate Video Tracks

- **内部类名**：`GenerateTracks`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_wanmove`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Generate Video Tracks` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 832；最小 16；最大 4096；步长 16 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 480；最小 16；最大 4096；步长 16 |
| `start_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized X coordinate (0-1) for start position. |
| `start_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized Y coordinate (0-1) for start position. |
| `end_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized X coordinate (0-1) for end position. |
| `end_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized Y coordinate (0-1) for end position. |
| `num_frames` | `INT` | required | 默认 81；最小 1；最大 1024 |
| `num_tracks` | `INT` | required | 默认 5；最小 1；最大 100 |
| `track_spread` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.025；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.001；源码提示：Normalized distance between tracks. Tracks are spread perpendicular to the motion direction. |
| `bezier` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Enable Bezier curve path using the mid point as control point. |
| `mid_x` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized X control point for Bezier curve. Only used when 'bezier' is enabled. |
| `mid_y` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.5；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Normalized Y control point for Bezier curve. Only used when 'bezier' is enabled. |
| `interpolation` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：Controls the timing/speed of movement along the path. |
| `track_mask` | `MASK` | optional | 源码提示：Optional mask to indicate visible frames. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `TRACKS` | `TRACKS` |
| `track_length` | `INT` |

<a id="model-conditioning-wan-scail"></a>

## model/conditioning/wan/scail

构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### Create SCAIL-2 Colored Mask

- **内部类名**：`SCAIL2ColoredMask`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_scail`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create SCAIL-2 Colored Mask` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `driving_track_data` | `SAM3_TRACK_DATA` | required | 源码提示：SAM3 track of the driving pose video. Will be rendered into the pose_video_mask output. |
| `object_indices` | `STRING` | required | 默认 ''；源码提示：Comma-separated list of person indices to include (e.g. '0,2,3'). Applied to both reference and pose video masks. Empty = all. |
| `sort_by` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'left_to_right'；源码提示：Order in which palette colors are assigned to the tracked objects (applied to both reference and pose video so each identity keeps the same color). Objects that appear in earlier frames always come first; within a frame, left_to_right = leftmost object (by centroid at first appearance) gets the first color, area = biggest object (by mask area at first appearance) gets the first color; none = keep SAM3's order. |
| `replacement_mode` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：False = Animation Mode (pose_video_mask has black background, reference_image_mask has white background). True = Replacement Mode (pose_video_mask has white background, reference_image_mask has black background). |
| `ref_track_data` | `SAM3_TRACK_DATA,MASK` | optional | 源码提示：SAM3 track of the reference image(s) (one identity per object, colored in batch order), or a plain MASK of the reference subject (rendered as a single identity). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `pose_video_mask` | `IMAGE` |
| `reference_image_mask` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

### WanSCAILToVideo

- **内部类名**：`WanSCAILToVideo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_scail`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `WanSCAILToVideo` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | — |
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 512；最小 32；最大 16384；步长 32 |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 896；最小 32；最大 16384；步长 32 |
| `length` | `INT` | required | 默认 81；最小 1；最大 16384；步长 4 |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 4096 |
| `pose_strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Strength of the pose latent. |
| `pose_start` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Start step of the pose conditioning. |
| `pose_end` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：End step of the pose conditioning. |
| `video_frame_offset` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；步长 1；源码提示：Cumulative output frame this chunk begins at. Wire from the previous chunk's video_frame_offset output. |
| `previous_frame_count` | `INT` | required | 默认 5；最小 1；最大 16384；步长 4；源码提示：Tail frames of previous_frames to anchor. SCAIL-2 trained at 5 (81-frame chunks, 76-frame step). |
| `pose_video` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Video used for pose conditioning. Will be downscaled to half the resolution of the main video. |
| `pose_video_mask` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：SCAIL-2 only. Colored per-identity SAM3 mask video at the same resolution as pose_video. |
| `replacement_mode` | `BOOLEAN` | optional | 默认 False；源码提示：SCAIL-2 only. False = Animation Mode (pose_video_mask should have black background). True = Replacement Mode (pose_video_mask should have white background). |
| `reference_image` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Reference image. The first image is the primary reference (composite all identities onto it). SCAIL-2: extra batch images are used as additional views (back view, close-up, occluded background), each needing a matching reference_image_mask in that identity's color. |
| `reference_image_mask` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：SCAIL-2 only. Colored reference mask, batch matching reference_image (first = primary reference mask, rest = identity masks for the additional reference_image). |
| `clip_vision_output` | `CLIP_VISION_OUTPUT` | optional | 源码提示：CLIP vision features for conditioning. Model is trained with stretch resize to aspect ratio. |
| `previous_frames` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：SCAIL-2 only. Full decoded output of the previous chunk. Only the last previous_frame_count are used as the extension anchor. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |
| `video_frame_offset` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

| `control_masks` | `MASK` | optional | — |
| `reference_image` | `IMAGE` | optional | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` |
| `latent` | `LATENT` |
| `trim_latent` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：构造或修改生成条件；注意正向/负向语义、生效区间和模型兼容性。

|---|---|---|---|
| `seconds` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 47.6；最小 1.0；最大 1000.0；步长 0.1 |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 4096；源码提示：The number of latent images in the batch. |

|---|---|
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

### Merge SeedVR2 Latents

- **内部类名**：`SeedVR2TemporalMerge`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_seedvr`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：合并、重组或复制批次数据；具体对象见节点名 `Merge SeedVR2 Latents`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Recombine sampled SeedVR2 latent temporal chunks into one latent, crossfading each overlap with a Hann window sized by the temporal_overlap wired from Split SeedVR2 Latent.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：The sampled temporal chunks in sequence order. |
| `temporal_overlap` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：The temporal_overlap output of Split SeedVR2 Latent. 0 = plain concatenation. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `latent` | `LATENT` |

**小白提醒**：处理潜空间数据；LATENT 不是可见图片，需要匹配 VAE 解码。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|

| `index` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 -16384；最大 16384；步长 1；源码提示：The starting latent frame index in the destination latent where the source latent frames will be placed. Negative values count from the end. |
| `source` | `LATENT` | optional | 源码提示：The source latent providing frames to insert into the destination latent. If not provided, the destination latent is returned unchanged. |

|---|---|
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

### Split SeedVR2 Latent

- **内部类名**：`SeedVR2TemporalChunk`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_seedvr`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：从输入中拆分或提取部分数据；具体对象见节点名 `Split SeedVR2 Latent`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Split a SeedVR2 video latent into overlapping temporal chunks small enough to sample one at a time within VRAM, wiring latents outputs to both Apply SeedVR2 Conditioning and the sampler latent input before recombining with Merge SeedVR2 Latents.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `latent` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：The VAE-encoded SeedVR2 latent to split. |
| `temporal_overlap` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 16384；源码提示：Latent frames shared between adjacent chunks and crossfaded at merge; 0 = no overlap. |
| `chunking_mode` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：manual = use frames_per_chunk exactly; auto = predict the largest chunk that fits free VRAM. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` |
| `temporal_overlap` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：处理潜空间数据；LATENT 不是可见图片，需要匹配 VAE 解码。

|---|---|
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

<a id="model-latent-triposplat"></a>

## model/latent/triposplat

处理潜空间数据；LATENT 不是可见图片，需要匹配 VAE 解码。

### TripoSplat Decode

- **内部类名**：`VAEDecodeTripoSplat`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_triposplat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：把编码数据解码成更接近最终媒体的表示；具体对象见节点名 `TripoSplat Decode`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Decode the sampled TripoSplat latent into a 3D gaussian splat. Modify the number of gaussians to vary the density.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `samples` | `LATENT` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | 源码提示：TripoSplat VAE decoder |
| `num_gaussians` | `INT` | required | 默认 262144；最小 32768；最大 1048576；步长 32；源码提示：Number of gaussians to produce (rounded to a multiple of 32). 262144 matches the octree's point density; higher oversamples the same points (denser, but no new detail) and costs proportionally more VRAM/time. |
| `seed` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 18446744073709551615；源码提示：Seeds the octree point sampler (global RNG) for deterministic decodes. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `splat` | `SPLAT` |

**小白提醒**：处理潜空间数据；LATENT 不是可见图片，需要匹配 VAE 解码。

### TripoSplat Sampling Preview

- **内部类名**：`TripoSplatSamplingPreview`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_triposplat`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：配置或执行扩散采样过程；具体对象见节点名 `TripoSplat Sampling Preview`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Patch the TripoSplat model for the standard Ksampler node to show a live decoded gaussian splat preview at each step.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | 源码提示：TripoSplat VAE decoder |
| `octree_level` | `INT` | required | 默认 5；最小 2；最大 8；源码提示：Octree depth for the preview decode (lower = cheaper/coarser). |
| `num_gaussians` | `INT` | required | 默认 16384；最小 1024；最大 262144；步长 32；源码提示：Number of gaussians to produce for the preview (rounded to a multiple of 32). |
| `yaw` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 90.0；最小 -360.0；最大 360.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Preview camera yaw in degrees. |
| `pitch` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 15.0；最小 -89.0；最大 89.0；步长 1.0；源码提示：Preview camera pitch in degrees. |
| `point_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 3；最小 1；最大 16；源码提示：Maximum splat radius in pixels. Each gaussian is sized from its scale and capped here; lower = finer/pointier, higher = chunkier. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：处理潜空间数据；LATENT 不是可见图片，需要匹配 VAE 解码。

### Load LoRA (Bypass) (For debugging)

- **内部类名**：`LoraLoaderBypass`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_lora_debug`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：加载对应资源并把它交给下游节点；具体对象见节点名 `Load LoRA (Bypass) (For debugging)`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Apply LoRA in bypass mode. Unlike regular LoRA, this doesn't modify model weights - instead it injects the LoRA computation during forward pass. Useful for training scenarios.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The diffusion model the LoRA will be applied to. |
| `clip` | `CLIP` | required | 源码提示：The CLIP model the LoRA will be applied to. |
| `lora_name` | `COMBO` | required | 选项由当前环境动态提供；源码提示：The name of the LoRA. |
| `strength_model` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 -100.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：How strongly to modify the diffusion model. This value can be negative. |
| `strength_clip` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 -100.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：How strongly to modify the CLIP model. This value can be negative. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |
| `CLIP` | `CLIP` |

**小白提醒**：加载模型组件；先核对文件目录、模型架构、精度和配套组件。

|---|---|---|---|
| `clip1` | `CLIP` | required | — |
| `clip2` | `CLIP` | required | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `CLIP` | `CLIP` |

<a id="model-patch"></a>

## model/patch

修改 MODEL 的推理行为；优先跟随对应模型模板，不要无目的叠加。

### Context Windows (Manual)

- **内部类名**：`ContextWindowsManual`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_context_windows`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Context Windows (Manual)` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Manually set context windows.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model to apply context windows to during sampling. |
| `context_length` | `INT` | required | 默认 16；最小 1；源码提示：The length of the context window. |
| `context_overlap` | `INT` | required | 默认 4；最小 0；源码提示：The overlap of the context window. |
| `context_schedule` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'standard_static'；源码提示：Step-dependent scheduling algorithm for context windows. |
| `context_stride` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；源码提示：The stride of the context window; only applicable to uniform schedules. |
| `closed_loop` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to close the context window loop; only applicable to looped schedules. |
| `fuse_method` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'pyramid'；源码提示：The method to use to fuse the context windows. |
| `dim` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 5；源码提示：The dimension to apply the context windows to. |
| `freenoise` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to apply FreeNoise noise shuffling, improves window blending. |
| `cond_retain_index_list` | `STRING` | required | 默认 ''；源码提示：List of latent indices to retain in the conditioning tensors for each window. For concat-style I2V models (e.g. Wan I2V, HunyuanVideo I2V, Cosmos I2V, SVD) the encoded start image lives in the c_concat conditioning channels; setting this to '0' will retain that start image content at sub-pos 0 of every window. |
| `split_conds_to_windows` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to split multiple conditionings (created by ConditionCombine) to each window based on region index. |
| `latent_retain_index_list` | `STRING` | required | 默认 ''；源码提示：List of latent indices to retain in the noise latent itself for each window. Use for workflows where reference content (e.g. a start image) lives directly in the noise latent rather than in separate conditioning channels (e.g. inplace-style I2V like LTXV, AnimateDiff). Independent of cond_retain_index_list. |
| `causal_window_fix` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Whether to add a causal fix frame to non-0-indexed context windows. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：修改 MODEL 的推理行为；优先跟随对应模型模板，不要无目的叠加。

### LTXV Context Windows

- **内部类名**：`LTXVContextWindows`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_context_windows`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `LTXV Context Windows` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Manually set context windows.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model to apply context windows to during sampling. |
| `context_length` | `INT` | required | 默认 145；最小 1；最大 16384；步长 8；源码提示：The length of the context window in real frames. Must be 8*n + 1. |
| `context_overlap` | `INT` | required | 默认 40；最小 0；步长 8；源码提示：The overlap of the context window in real frames. |
| `context_schedule` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'standard_uniform'；源码提示：Step-dependent scheduling algorithm for context windows. |
| `context_stride` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；源码提示：The stride of the context window; only applicable to uniform schedules. |
| `closed_loop` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to close the context window loop; only applicable to looped schedules. |
| `fuse_method` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'pyramid'；源码提示：The method to use to fuse the context windows. |
| `freenoise` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Whether to apply FreeNoise noise shuffling, improves window blending. |
| `retain_first_frame` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Retain the first latent frame in every context window (may help retain initial reference). |
| `split_conds_to_windows` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Whether to split multiple conditionings (created by ConditionCombine) to each window based on region index. |

| 输出 | 类型 |

| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

### ChromaRadianceOptions

- **内部类名**：`ChromaRadianceOptions`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_chroma_radiance`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `ChromaRadianceOptions` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Allows setting advanced options for the Chroma Radiance model.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `preserve_wrapper` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：When enabled, will delegate to an existing model function wrapper if it exists. Generally should be left enabled. |
| `start_sigma` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；源码提示：First sigma that these options will be in effect. |
| `end_sigma` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；源码提示：Last sigma that these options will be in effect. |
| `nerf_tile_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 -1；最小 -1；源码提示：Allows overriding the default NeRF tile size. -1 means use the default (32). 0 means use non-tiling mode (may require a lot of VRAM). |
| `force_sequential_txt_ids` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Force usage of sequential text token IDs instead of zeroes. Should be used for checkpoints from 2026-05-22 to 2026-06-01 that are trained in this way but do not contain the __sequential__ key in the state dict. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：修改 MODEL 的推理行为；优先跟随对应模型模板，不要无目的叠加。

<a id="model-patch-hidream"></a>

## model/patch/hidream

修改 MODEL 的推理行为；优先跟随对应模型模板，不要无目的叠加。

### HiDream-O1 Patch Seam Smoothing

- **内部类名**：`HiDreamO1PatchSeamSmoothing`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_hidream_o1`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `HiDream-O1 Patch Seam Smoothing` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Average the model output across multiple shifted patch-grid positions during the late portion of sampling. Cancels seams.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `start_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.8；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Sampling progress (0=start, 1=end) at which the blend turns ON. |
| `end_percent` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Sampling progress at which the blend turns OFF. |
| `pattern` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'single_shift'；源码提示：Shift layout. single_shift: one pass at the natural patch grid + others offset. symmetric: all passes off-grid, shifts split around origin. |
| `passes` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 '2'；源码提示：Number of passes per gated step. 2/4 = fixed. ramp_*: pass count increases as sampling approaches end (more smoothing where seams are most visible). |
| `blend` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'average'；源码提示：average: equal-weight mean. window: Hann-windowed weighting favoring each pass away from its patch boundaries. median: per-pixel median, rejects wraparound-outlier passes. |
| `strength` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Interpolation between the natural-grid pred (0) and the averaged result (1). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：修改 MODEL 的推理行为；优先跟随对应模型模板，不要无目的叠加。

<a id="model-sampling"></a>

## model/sampling

参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

### KSampler

- **内部类名**：`KSampler`
- **源码模块**：`nodes`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：配置或执行扩散采样过程；具体对象见节点名 `KSampler`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Uses the provided model, positive and negative conditioning to denoise the latent image.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model used for denoising the input latent. |
| `seed` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 18446744073709551615；源码提示：The random seed used for creating the noise. |
| `steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 20；最小 1；最大 10000；源码提示：The number of steps used in the denoising process. |
| `cfg` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 8.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：The Classifier-Free Guidance scale balances creativity and adherence to the prompt. Higher values result in images more closely matching the prompt however too high values will negatively impact quality. |
| `sampler_name` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'euler'、'euler_cfg_pp'、'euler_ancestral'、'euler_ancestral_cfg_pp'、'heun'、'heunpp2'、'exp_heun_2_x0'、'exp_heun_2_x0_sde'、'dpm_2'、'dpm_2_ancestral'，另有 34 项；源码提示：The algorithm used when sampling, this can affect the quality, speed, and style of the generated output. |
| `scheduler` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'simple'、'sgm_uniform'、'karras'、'exponential'、'ddim_uniform'、'beta'、'normal'、'linear_quadratic'、'kl_optimal'；源码提示：The scheduler controls how noise is gradually removed to form the image. |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | 源码提示：The conditioning describing the attributes you want to include in the image. |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | 源码提示：The conditioning describing the attributes you want to exclude from the image. |
| `latent_image` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：The latent image to denoise. |
| `denoise` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：The amount of denoising applied, lower values will maintain the structure of the initial image allowing for image to image sampling. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

### KSampler (Advanced)

- **内部类名**：`KSamplerAdvanced`
- **源码模块**：`nodes`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：配置或执行扩散采样过程；具体对象见节点名 `KSampler (Advanced)`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `add_noise` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'enable'、'disable' |
| `noise_seed` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 18446744073709551615 |
| `steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 20；最小 1；最大 10000 |
| `cfg` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 8.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.1 |
| `sampler_name` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'euler'、'euler_cfg_pp'、'euler_ancestral'、'euler_ancestral_cfg_pp'、'heun'、'heunpp2'、'exp_heun_2_x0'、'exp_heun_2_x0_sde'、'dpm_2'、'dpm_2_ancestral'，另有 34 项 |
| `scheduler` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'simple'、'sgm_uniform'、'karras'、'exponential'、'ddim_uniform'、'beta'、'normal'、'linear_quadratic'、'kl_optimal' |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `negative` | `CONDITIONING` | required | — |
| `latent_image` | `LATENT` | required | — |
| `start_at_step` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 10000 |
| `end_at_step` | `INT` | required | 默认 10000；最小 0；最大 10000 |
| `return_with_leftover_noise` | `COMBO` | required | 选项：'disable'、'enable' |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `LATENT` | `LATENT` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。


<a id="model-sampling-custom"></a>

## model/sampling/custom

参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

### Adaptive Projected Guidance

- **内部类名**：`APG`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_apg`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Adaptive Projected Guidance` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `eta` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 -10.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Controls the scale of the parallel guidance vector. Default CFG behavior at a setting of 1. |
| `norm_threshold` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 5.0；最小 0.0；最大 50.0；步长 0.1；源码提示：Normalize guidance vector to this value, normalization disable at a setting of 0. |
| `momentum` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -5.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Controls a running average of guidance during diffusion, disabled at a setting of 0. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `MODEL` | `MODEL` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

| `solver_type` | `COMBO` | required | — |

| `eta` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Stochastic strength of SDEs. When eta=0, they reduce to deterministic ODE. Large eta may cause invalid outputs. If this occurs, try decreasing this value. |
| `s_noise` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 100.0；步长 0.01 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `SAMPLER` | `SAMPLER` |

### SamplerLCM

- **内部类名**：`SamplerLCM`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_advanced_samplers`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：配置或执行扩散采样过程；具体对象见节点名 `SamplerLCM`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：LCM sampler with tunable per-step noise. s_noise is a multiplier on the model's training noise scale

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `s_noise` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 64.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Per-step noise multiplier at the first step (1.0 = match training). |
| `s_noise_end` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0.0；最大 64.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Per-step noise multiplier at the last step. Set equal to s_noise for a constant schedule. |
| `noise_clip_std` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；最大 10.0；步长 0.01；源码提示：Clamp per-step noise to +/- N*std. 0 disables. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `SAMPLER` | `SAMPLER` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

- **用途提示**：生成或调整采样使用的噪声时间表；具体对象见节点名 `SDTurboScheduler`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | — |
| `steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 10 |
| `denoise` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 1.0；最小 0；最大 1.0；步长 0.01 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `SIGMAS` | `SIGMAS` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

### VPScheduler

- **内部类名**：`VPScheduler`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_custom_sampler`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：生成或调整采样使用的噪声时间表；具体对象见节点名 `VPScheduler`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 20；最小 1；最大 10000 |
| `beta_d` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 19.9；最小 0.0；最大 5000.0；步长 0.01 |
| `beta_min` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.1；最小 0.0；最大 5000.0；步长 0.01 |
| `eps_s` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.001；最小 0.0；最大 1.0；步长 0.0001 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `SIGMAS` | `SIGMAS` |

**小白提醒**：参与噪声调度、引导或采样；固定 seed 后一次只改一个参数。

<a id="model-training"></a>

## model/training

训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Load Training Dataset

- **内部类名**：`LoadTrainingDataset`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：加载对应资源并把它交给下游节点；具体对象见节点名 `Load Training Dataset`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Load encoded training dataset (latents + conditioning) from disk for use in training.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder_name` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：Saved dataset to load, from the datasets directory. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Make Training Dataset

- **内部类名**：`MakeTrainingDataset`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Make Training Dataset` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Encode images with VAE and texts with CLIP to create a training dataset of latents and conditionings.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：List of images to encode. |
| `vae` | `VAE` | required | 源码提示：VAE model for encoding images to latents. |
| `clip` | `CLIP` | required | 源码提示：CLIP model for encoding text to conditioning. |
| `texts` | `STRING` | optional | 源码提示：List of text captions. Can be length n (matching images), 1 (repeated for all), or omitted (uses empty string). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Plot Loss Graph

- **内部类名**：`LossGraphNode`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_train`
- **标记**：实验性；输出节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Plot Loss Graph` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `loss` | `LOSS_MAP` | required | 源码提示：Loss map from training node. |
| `filename_prefix` | `STRING` | required | 默认 'loss_graph'；源码提示：Prefix for the saved loss graph image. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| — | 无公开输出 |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Resolution Bucket

- **内部类名**：`ResolutionBucket`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Resolution Bucket` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Group latents and conditionings into buckets

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：List of latent dicts to bucket by resolution. |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` | required | 源码提示：List of conditioning lists (must match latents length). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Save Training Dataset

- **内部类名**：`SaveTrainingDataset`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性；输出节点
- **用途提示**：把输入结果写入输出目录或指定文件；具体对象见节点名 `Save Training Dataset`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Save encoded training dataset (latents + conditioning) to disk for efficient loading during training.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `latents` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：List of latent dicts from MakeTrainingDataset. |
| `conditioning` | `CONDITIONING` | required | 源码提示：List of conditioning lists from MakeTrainingDataset. |
| `folder_name` | `STRING` | required | 默认 'training_dataset'；源码提示：Name of folder to save the dataset into, inside the datasets directory. Subfolders like 'project/run1' are allowed. |
| `shard_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1000；最小 1；最大 100000；源码提示：Number of samples per shard file. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| — | 无公开输出 |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

### Train LoRA

- **内部类名**：`TrainLoraNode`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_train`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Train LoRA` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model` | `MODEL` | required | 源码提示：The model to train the LoRA on. |
| `latents` | `LATENT` | required | 源码提示：The Latents to use for training, serve as dataset/input of the model. |
| `positive` | `CONDITIONING` | required | 源码提示：The positive conditioning to use for training. |
| `batch_size` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 10000；源码提示：The batch size to use for training. |
| `grad_accumulation_steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 1024；源码提示：The number of gradient accumulation steps to use for training. |
| `steps` | `INT` | required | 默认 16；最小 1；最大 100000；源码提示：The number of steps to train the LoRA for. |
| `learning_rate` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0005；最小 1e-07；最大 1.0；步长 1e-07；源码提示：The learning rate to use for training. |
| `rank` | `INT` | required | 默认 8；最小 1；最大 128；源码提示：The rank of the LoRA layers. |
| `optimizer` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'AdamW'；源码提示：The optimizer to use for training. |
| `loss_function` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'MSE'；源码提示：The loss function to use for training. |
| `seed` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 18446744073709551615；源码提示：The seed to use for training (used in generator for LoRA weight initialization and noise sampling) |
| `training_dtype` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'bf16'；源码提示：The dtype to use for training. 'none' preserves the model's native compute dtype instead of overriding it. For fp16 models, GradScaler is automatically enabled. |
| `lora_dtype` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'bf16'；源码提示：The dtype to use for lora. |
| `quantized_backward` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：When using training_dtype 'none' and training on quantized model, doing backward with quantized matmul when enabled. |
| `algorithm` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'LoRA'；源码提示：The algorithm to use for training. |
| `gradient_checkpointing` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 True；源码提示：Use gradient checkpointing for training. |
| `checkpoint_depth` | `INT` | required | 默认 1；最小 1；最大 5；源码提示：Depth level for gradient checkpointing. |
| `offloading` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Offload model weights to CPU during training to save GPU memory. |
| `existing_lora` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 '[None]'；源码提示：The existing LoRA to append to. Set to None for new LoRA. |
| `bucket_mode` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Enable resolution bucket mode. When enabled, expects pre-bucketed latents from ResolutionBucket node. |
| `bypass_mode` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：Enable bypass mode for training. When enabled, adapters are applied via forward hooks instead of weight modification. Useful for quantized models where weights cannot be directly modified. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `lora` | `LORA_MODEL` |
| `loss_map` | `LOSS_MAP` |
| `steps` | `INT` |

**小白提醒**：训练或构建数据集；运行前确认数据、输出目录、磁盘空间和训练配置。

| `max_length` | `INT` | required | 默认 77；最小 1；最大 10000；源码提示：Maximum text length. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `texts` | `STRING` |

**小白提醒**：处理文本或结构化提示词；注意空格、大小写、正则表达式和输出格式。

### Create Bounding Boxes

- **内部类名**：`CreateBoundingBoxes`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_bounding_boxes`
- **标记**：实验性；输出节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create Bounding Boxes` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Draw bounding boxes in a canvas. Outputs Ideogram prompt elements, pixel-space bounding boxes, and a preview image.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `width` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 16384；步长 16；源码提示：Width of the canvas and the pixel grid for the bounding boxes. |
| `height` | `INT` | required | 默认 1024；最小 64；最大 16384；步长 16；源码提示：Height of the canvas and the pixel grid for the bounding boxes. |
| `editor_state` | `BOUNDING_BOXES` | required | 默认 []；源码提示：Draw bounding boxes and set each box type, text, description, color palette. Start with background element first and foreground last. |
| `background` | `IMAGE` | optional | 源码提示：Optional image used as background in the canvas and preview. |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX,ARRAY,STRING` | optional | 源码提示：Bounding boxes, elements, or a JSON string to initialize the canvas. A new upstream value initializes the canvas; edits made on the canvas take priority and are kept until the upstream value changes again. |
| `last_incoming` | `BOUNDING_BOXES` | optional | 默认 []；源码提示：Internal state managed by the canvas: the upstream bboxes value that last initialized it. Leave empty to re-initialize the canvas from the bboxes input on the next run. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `preview` | `IMAGE` |
| `bboxes` | `BOUNDING_BOX` |
| `elements` | `ARRAY` |

**小白提醒**：提供数字、逻辑、列表或通用辅助数据，常用于参数化工作流。

## video

处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Create Video

- **内部类名**：`CreateVideo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_video`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Create Video` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Create a video from images.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：The images to create a video from. |
| `fps` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 30.0；最小 1.0；最大 120.0；步长 1.0 |
| `audio` | `AUDIO` | optional | 源码提示：The audio to add to the video. |
| `bit_depth` | `COMBO` | optional | 默认 'auto'；源码提示：Auto uses 8-bit for sRGB and 10-bit for HDR. Explicit 8-bit and 10-bit choices are independent of colorspace. |
| `color_space` | `COMBO` | optional | 默认 'sRGB'；源码提示：Colorspace of the input images. HDR selects BT.2020/HLG and HDR PQ selects BT.2020/PQ. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `VIDEO` | `VIDEO` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：加载对应资源并把它交给下游节点；具体对象见节点名 `Load Video-Text (from Folder)`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Load a dataset of pairs of videos and text captions from a specified folder and return them as a list. Supported formats: MP4, AVI, MOV, WEBM, MKV, FLV.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder` | `COMBO` | required | 源码提示：The folder containing video files and .txt captions. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `videos` | `VIDEO` |
| `texts` | `STRING` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Run Frame Interpolation Model

- **内部类名**：`FrameInterpolate`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_frame_interpolation`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：执行 `Run Frame Interpolation Model` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `interp_model` | `INTERP_MODEL` | required | — |
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | — |
| `multiplier` | `INT` | required | 默认 2；最小 2；最大 16 |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `IMAGE` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Sample Video Frame

- **内部类名**：`VideoFrameSample`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_dataset`
- **标记**：实验性
- **用途提示**：执行 `Sample Video Frame` 对应的专用处理；以下输入输出来自当前源码定义。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Sample a fixed number of frames from a video using various strategies.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `video` | `VIDEO` | required | 源码提示：Input video. |
| `num_frames` | `INT` | required | 默认 16；最小 1；最大 9999；源码提示：Number of frames to sample. |
| `strategy` | `COMBO` | required | 默认 'uniform'；源码提示：uniform: evenly spaced, head: first N, tail: last N, random: random sorted. |
| `seed` | `INT` | required | 默认 0；最小 0；最大 18446744073709551615；源码提示：Random seed (only used with 'random' strategy). |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `video` | `VIDEO` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Save Video

- **内部类名**：`SaveVideo`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_video`
- **标记**：输出节点
- **用途提示**：把输入结果写入输出目录或指定文件；具体对象见节点名 `Save Video`。
- **源码说明（原文）**：Saves the input videos to your ComfyUI output directory.

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `video` | `VIDEO` | required | 源码提示：The video to save. |
| `filename_prefix` | `STRING` | required | 默认 'video/ComfyUI'；源码提示：The prefix for the file to save. This may include formatting information such as %date:yyyy-MM-dd% or %Empty Latent Image.width% to include values from nodes. |
| `format` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | required | 源码提示：The output container. Auto uses MP4 for Auto/H.264 and WebM for AV1. MP4, MKV, and WebM select a specific container. |
| `codec` | `COMFY_DYNAMICCOMBO_V3` | optional | 源码提示：The output video codec. Auto preserves a compatible source stream. H.264 and AV1 re-encoding support SDR, HDR (HLG), and HDR PQ. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `video` | `VIDEO` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Save WEBM

- **内部类名**：`SaveWEBM`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_video`
- **标记**：实验性；输出节点
- **用途提示**：把输入结果写入输出目录或指定文件；具体对象见节点名 `Save WEBM`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` | required | 源码提示：RGBA images are saved with their alpha channel as transparency (vp9 codec only). |
| `filename_prefix` | `STRING` | required | 默认 'ComfyUI' |
| `codec` | `COMBO` | required | — |
| `fps` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 24.0；最小 0.01；最大 1000.0；步长 0.01 |
| `crf` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 32.0；最小 0；最大 63.0；步长 1；源码提示：Higher crf means lower quality with a smaller file size, lower crf means higher quality higher filesize. |
| `prompt` | `PROMPT` | hidden | — |
| `extra_pnginfo` | `EXTRA_PNGINFO` | hidden | — |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `images` | `IMAGE` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

### Trim Video

- **内部类名**：`Video Slice`
- **源码模块**：`comfy_extras.nodes_video`
- **标记**：普通节点
- **用途提示**：裁剪输入的空间范围或时间范围；具体对象见节点名 `Trim Video`。

| 输入 | 类型 | 必需性 | 默认值/范围/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `video` | `VIDEO` | required | — |
| `start_time` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 -100000.0；最大 100000.0；步长 0.001；源码提示：Start time in seconds |
| `duration` | `FLOAT` | required | 默认 0.0；最小 0.0；步长 0.001；源码提示：Duration in seconds, or 0 for unlimited duration |
| `strict_duration` | `BOOLEAN` | required | 默认 False；源码提示：If True, when the specified duration is not possible, an error will be raised. |

| 输出 | 类型 |
|---|---|
| `VIDEO` | `VIDEO` |

**小白提醒**：处理视频；重点核对帧率、帧数、尺寸、时长和模型的时序限制。

## 更新本索引

在仓库根目录执行：

```bash
python docs/01-新手入门/ComfyUI-系统学习手册/tools/generate_node_reference.py
```

生成器只读取 ComfyUI 源码的节点元数据，并覆盖本文件。默认查找仓库根目录下被 Git 忽略的 `ComfyUI/`；也可用 `--comfy-root /path/to/ComfyUI` 指定其他源码目录。
